Your job is to evaluate new\n    63→ESS-DIVE dataset packages and, where appropriate, produce:\n    64→  (a) a Python code block to harmonize the dataset into the project schema, and\n    65→  (b) a JSON mapping entry documenting the harmonization.\n    66→\n    67→You follow an established workflow and strict schema. Use only the inputs it permits inside the run\n    75→workspace; do not use parent directories, absolute paths outside the workspace,\n    76→network services, APIs, or external lookup sources. Write every deliverable under\n    77→`output/`. The held-out dataset's canonical index is listed in `MANIFEST.json` and\n    78→must be retained in its mapping entry; do not assign a new sequential index. If an\n    79→input is missing, document the limitation rather than retrieving it externally.\n    80→\n    81→## SECTION 1: TARGET SCHEMA\n    82→\n    83→Every harmonized CSV must produce these columns (and no others):\n    84→\n    85→```python\n    86→datetime_UTC                          # ISO-8601 string, UTC\n    87→site_id                               # string\n    88→depth_m                               # float, meters below surface\n    89→replicate                             # int or np.nan\n    90→is_timeseries                         # boolean\n    91→interval_min                          # float or np.nan\n    92→volumetric_water_content_m3_m3        # float or np.nan\n    93→gravimetric_water_content_gH2O_gs     # float or np.nan\n    94→water_potential_kPa                   # float or np.nan\n    95→```\n    96→\n    97→**qc_flag vocabulary:**\n    98→- `d1` = depth is approximated from a reported range\n    99→- `g1` = coordinates from an approved location registry supplied in the workspace\n   100→- `g2` = coordinates not available from any source\n   101→\n   102→**Units:**\n   103→- depth → meters (m)\n   104→- VWC → m³ m⁻³ (convert from % by dividing by 100)\n   105→- GWC → g H₂O g⁻¹ soil\n   106→- water_potential → kPa (negative float; convert from MPa × 1000, from bar × 101.325)\n   107→\n   108→## SECTION 2: JSON MAPPING ENTRY SCHEMA\n   109→\n   110→Each dataset gets one entry in `sm_data_harmonization_mapping.json`:\n   111→\n   112→```json\n   113→{\n   114→  \"index\": <integer>,\n   115→  \"dataset_identifier\": \"<ESS-DIVE package ID>\",\n   116→  \"doi\": \"<DOI>\",\n   117→  \"archive_repository\": \"ESS-DIVE\",\n   118→  \"data_payload_files\": [\"<filename>\", ...] or null,\n   119→  \"location_metadata_files\": [\"<filename>\", ...] or null,\n   120→  \"sensor_metadata_files\": [\"<filename>\", ...] or null,\n   121→  \"harmonization_mappings\": {\n   122→    \"<target_variable>\": {\n   123→      \"pattern_1\": {\n   124→        \"source_pattern\": \"<regex or column name>\",\n   125→        \"source_files\": [\"<filename>\", ...],\n   126→        \"destination_variable\": \"<harmonized variable name>\",\n   127→        \"transformation\": \"<description>\",\n   128→        \"unit_conversion\": \"<description or null>\"\n   129→      }\n   130→    }\n   131→  }\n   132→}\n   133→```\n   134→\n   135→**For excluded datasets:**\n   136→- Set `\"data_payload_files\": null`\n   137→- Set `\"harmonization_mappings\": \"EXCLUDED: <plain-language reason>\"`\n   138→\n   139→## SECTION 3: DECISION RULES\n   140→\n   141→Apply these rules in order when evaluating a new dataset:\n   142→\n   143→**RULE 1 — DUPLICATE / SUPERSEDED CHECK**\n   144→\n   145→If this package is a prior version of, or contains data duplicated in, another already-included package: EXCLUDE.\n   146→\n   147→Reason format: `\"Superseded by <package_id>\"` or `\"Data duplicated in <package_id>\"`\n   148→\n   149→**RULE 2 — MEASUREMENT TYPE CHECK**\n   150→\n   151→The dataset must contain direct observations of at least one of:\n   152→- Volumetric water content (VWC)\n   153→- Gravimetric water content (GWC)\n   154→- Soil water potential / matric potential\n   155→\n   156→Exclude if measurements are:\n   157→- Modeled/estimated (e.g., from a water balance or pedotransfer function)\n   158→- Borehole moisture proxies (e.g., estimated from geophysical logs)\n   159→- Derived indices only (e.g., drought index, normalized difference)\n   160→\n   161→Reason format: `\"Does not contain direct soil moisture observations: <detail>\"`\n   162→\n   163→**RULE 3 — EXPERIMENTAL MANIPULATION CHECK**\n   164→\n   165→Flag (do not automatically exclude) if:\n   166→- Dataset is from a warming, irrigation, or other manipulation experiment\n   167→- Manipulation is clearly confounded with natural moisture signal\n   168→\n   169→Document the manipulation in the JSON \"transformation\" field. Ask the operator if uncertain whether to include.\n   170→\n   171→**RULE 4 — EXTRACTABLE PAYLOAD CHECK**\n   172→\n   173→At least one file must contain a parseable time-stamped or dated measurement table. Exclude if only summary statistics, figures, or non-machine-readable formats are available with no structured data.\n   174→\n   175→Reason format: `\"No machine-readable measurement payload available\"`\n   176→\n   177→**RULE 5 — MINIMUM METADATA CHECK**\n   178→\n   179→The dataset must have at least one of:\n   180→- Site coordinates in the payload, ancillary file, cached metadata, or an approved\n   181→  location registry supplied in the workspace\n   182→- A site identifier traceable to a known location\n   183→\n   184→If coordinates are entirely unresolvable, include with `qc_flag = \"g2\"` and document the gap.\n   185→\n   186→## SECTION 4: TIME SERIES INFERENCE RULES\n   187→\n   188→Set `is_timeseries = True` if any of the following:\n   189→- Multiple observations exist per site+depth with varying timestamps\n   190→- The dataset explicitly describes a sensor deployment / logger output\n   191→- Column names or README describe a monitoring frequency or interval\n   192→\n   193→Set `is_timeseries = False` if:\n   194→- Each site+depth has only one observation (e.g., a sampling campaign)\n   195→- Timestamps are campaign dates, not continuous sensor output\n   196→- README/methods describe discrete sampling\n   197→\n   198→Set `interval_min = np.nan` if `is_timeseries = False` or interval is irregular.\n   199→\n   200→Infer `interval_min` from median timestamp difference if not explicitly stated:\n   201→\n   202→```python\n   203→x = x.sort_values([\"site_id\", \"depth_m\", \"datetime_UTC\"])\n   204→x[\"interval_min\"] = x.groupby([\"site_id\", \"depth_m\"])[\"datetime_UTC\"] \\\n   205→    .diff().dt.total_seconds() / 60.0\n   206→```\n   207→\n   208→## SECTION 5: LOCATION RESOLUTION PRIORITY ORDER\n   209→\n   210→Resolve site coordinates using this fallback hierarchy:\n   211→\n   212→1. Coordinates in the data payload file itself\n   213→2. Coordinates in a package ancillary file (e.g., site_metadata.csv, locations.csv, README table)\n   214→3. Coordinates in the package-level ESS-DIVE metadata record\n   215→4. An approved location registry supplied in the workspace → set `qc_flag = \"g1\"`\n   216→5. Not resolvable → set `lat/lon = np.nan`, `qc_flag = \"g2\"`\n   217→\n   218→## SECTION 6: PYTHON CODE CONVENTIONS\n   219→\n   220→All generated Python code must conform to the following conventions.\n   221→\n   222→**IMPORTS (always include at top of each block if standalone):**\n   223→\n   224→```python\n   225→from __future__ import annotations\n   226→from pathlib import Path\n   227→import re\n   228→import numpy as np\n   229→import pandas as pd\n   230→```\n   231→\n   232→**FILE PATHS:**\n   233→\n   234→```python\n   235→BASE_DIR = Path(\"inputs/raw\")\n   236→OUT_DIR = Path(\"output\")\n   237→MAP_JSON_PATH = OUT_DIR / \"sm_data_harmonization_mapping.json\"\n   238→\n   239→# For specific dataset\n   240→idx = <dataset_index>\n   241→```\n   242→\n   243→**HELPER FUNCTIONS (reference from existing script):**\n   244→\n   245→- `dsid(idx: int) -> str` - get dataset identifier\n   246→- `ds_path(idx: int) -> Path` - get dataset directory path\n   247→- `read_ds_csv(idx: int, filename: str, **kwargs) -> pd.DataFrame` - read dataset CSV\n   248→- `parse_local_to_utc(series: pd.Series, fmt: str | None, tz: str) -> pd.Series` - convert local time to UTC\n   249→- `interval_min(s: pd.Series) -> pd.Series` - calculate interval in minutes\n   250→- `utm32613_to_latlon(df: pd.DataFrame, e_col: str, n_col: str) -> pd.DataFrame` - convert UTM to lat/lon\n   251→- `ensure_harmonized_cols(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame` - enforce column schema\n   252→- `add_loc_qc(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame` - add location QC flags\n   253→\n   254→**READING FILES:**\n   255→\n   256→```python\n   257→ddf = read_ds_csv(idx, \"<filename>\")\n   258→mdf = read_ds_csv(idx, \"<location_metadata_file>\")\n   259→```\n   260→\n   261→**RENAMING COLUMNS:**\n   262→\n   263→```python\n   264→x = df.rename(columns={\n   265→    \"<source_col>\": \"<harmonized_col>\",\n   266→    ...\n   267→})\n   268→```\n   269→\n   270→**WIDE-TO-LONG RESHAPING:**\n   271→\n   272→```python\n   273→long = df.melt(\n   274→    id_vars=[\"<id_col1>\", \"<id_col2>\"],\n   275→    value_vars=[\"<var1>\", \"<var2>\"],\n   276→    var_name=\"<source_variable>\",\n   277→    value_name=\"<target_variable>\"\n   278→)\n   279→\n   280→# When depth is embedded in column names:\n   281→long[\"depth_m\"] = pd.to_numeric(\n   282→    long[\"<source_variable>\"].str.extract(r\"(\\d+\\.?\\d*)\")[0],\n   283→    errors=\"coerce\"\n   284→) / 100  # adjust divisor per source units\n   285→```\n   286→\n   287→**TIMESTAMP PARSING (always convert to UTC):**\n   288→\n   289→```python\n   290→x[\"datetime_UTC\"] = parse_local_to_utc(\n   291→    x[\"<source_timestamp_col>\"],\n   292→    \"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S\",\n   293→    \"America/Denver\"\n   294→)\n   295→```\n   296→\n   297→**UNIT CONVERSIONS:**\n   298→\n   299→```python\n   300→# % VWC → m³ m⁻³\n   301→df[\"volumetric_water_content_m3_m3\"] = pd.to_numeric(\n   302→    df[\"<source_col>\"], errors=\"coerce\"\n   303→) / 100.0\n   304→\n   305→# MPa → kPa\n   306→df[\"water_potential_kPa\"] = pd.to_numeric(\n   307→    df[\"<source_col>\"], errors=\"coerce\"\n   308→) * 1000.0\n   309→\n   310→# bar → kPa\n   311→df[\"water_potential_kPa\"] = pd.to_numeric(\n   312→    df[\"<source_col>\"], errors=\"coerce\"\n   313→) * 101.325\n   314→```\n   315→\n   316→**ADDING CONSTANT METADATA COLUMNS:**\n   317→\n   318→```python\n   319→x[\"is_timeseries\"] = True          # or False\n   320→x[\"interval_min\"] = <float>        # or np.nan\n   321→x[\"replicate\"] = 1                 # or np.nan if not in source\n   322→x[\"qc_flag\"] = np.nan              # or \"g1\", \"g2\", \"d1\"\n   323→```\n   324→\n   325→**COLUMN ORDER ENFORCEMENT (always final step):**\n   326→\n   327→```python\n   328→df_harmonized = ensure_harmonized_cols(x)\n   329→```\n   330→\n   331→**OUTPUT:**\n   332→\n   333→```python\n   334→harmonized_data.append(df_harmonized)\n   335→harmonized_ids.append(dsid(idx))\n   336→```\n   337→\n   338→**BLOCK HEADER COMMENT (always precede each dataset block):**\n   339→\n   340→```python\n   341→# %%\n   342→# =============================================================\n   343→# Dataset <N>\n   344→# =============================================================\n   345→```\n   346→\n   347→If a dataset is EXCLUDED, emit only the header comment with exclusion reason.\n   348→\n   349→## SECTION 7: LOCATION CODE CONVENTIONS\n   350→\n   351→Location metadata is Zone 13N: 456789 E, 4445678 N`\n   256→\n   257→**Source 5: Supplied location registry**\n   258→\n   259→If an approved location registry is supplied inside the current workspace, it may\n   260→be Do not retrieve a\n   261→registry externally during a fold evaluation.\n   262→\n   263→**Source 6: Unresolvable**\n   264→\n   265→If truly no location information:\n   266→- Set `qc_flag = \"g2\"` recommendation\n   267→- Include with missing coordinates (to be reviewed)\n   268→\n   269→## SECTION 6: EXPERIMENTAL MANIPULATION DETECTION\n   270→\n   271→Scan for experimental manipulation keywords in:\n   272→- Package title\n   273→- Package description\n   274→- README content\n   275→- Column names\n   276→- File names\n   277→\n   278→**Exclusion keywords (likely manipulated):**\n   279→- `warming`, `heated`, `heat`, `warming treatment`\n   280→- `irrigation`, `water addition`, `precipitation manipulation`\n   281→- `fertiliz`, `nutrient addition`, `N addition`\n   282→- `elevated CO2`, `eCO2`\n   283→- `drought`, `rainout`, `precipitation exclusion`\n   284→- `treatment`, `control`, `experimental`\n   285→\n   286→**Ambiguous keywords (flag for review):**\n   287→- `burn`, `fire` (may be post-fire recovery, not manipulation)\n   288→- `harvest`, `thinning` (may be disturbance study)\n   289→- `gradient` (may be natural gradient vs experimental)\n   290→\n   291→**Detection logic:**\n   292→\n   293→If manipulation keywords found:\n   294→1. 303→**Time series indicators:**\n   304→- Multiple timestamp values per site/depth combination\n   305→- Regular timestamp intervals (hourly, daily, etc.)\n   306→- Keywords: `continuous`, `logger`, `sensor`, `automated`, `monitoring`\n   307→- File size suggests many observations (>1000 rows)\n   308→- Timestamp precision to hours/minutes/seconds\n   309→\n   310→**Discrete sampling lat/lon points using Haversine formula\"\"\"\n   168→        if any(pd.isna([lon1, lat1, lon2, lat2])):\n   169→            return np.inf\n   170→\n   171→        # Haversine formula\n   172→        R This approximation is\n  covered by the `d1` flag.\n\n## site_id and location resolution\n\n- `site_id` = `Location_FieldSite_Plot` (e.g. `East_River_Pumphouse_Hillslope_A1`).\n  `Field_Site` alone was rejected as too coarse: within a single Field_Site the\n  reported plots span up to ~2 km.\n- Coordinates come from the payload's `Latitude`/`Longitude` columns\n  (Location Resolution priority 1).
